# BROAD GLOSSARY OF VERIFIED SCRIPTURES

## *HALLOWED BE THY NAME* — every referenced verse, fetched and checked word-for-word

**206 verses banked for this work.** Versions: Old Testament: LXX Brenton exact (KJV only where the article's own voice is KJV). New Testament: 1599 Geneva.
Divided by the seventeen Seeker questions, exactly as the book is divided. Verified and double-checked 2 Nov 2026.

## *THE BIBLICAL SABBATH* — every referenced verse, fetched and checked word-for-word

**171 verses banked for this work.** Versions: Old Testament: NETS exact (LES where meaningfully different). New Testament: ancient codexes, codex named per quote (Sinaiticus dominant).
Verified 3–4 Nov 2026. Proposed corrections (the article's OT blockquotes are smoothed NETS rather than NETS-exact) await the word `apply`. Codex-mismatch and second-pass items are marked in place.

**377 verses banked in total.** Statuses: **CONFIRMED** = word-for-word · **EXCERPT** = word-for-word, partial · **DIFFERS** = wording differs (exact text of the specified version banked, proposal ready) · **PARAPHRASE** = reworded · ANCHOR = referenced, not quoted · RECAP = re-cited in prose. Nothing was changed in any manuscript; every ruling awaits the word `apply`.

## THE SABBATH OF SCRIPTURE — 3. The Manna Story (Exodus 16)

### Exodus 16:4 — DIFFERS (MINOR)

## THE SABBATH OF SCRIPTURE — 8. Jesus and the Sabbath

### Luke 4:16 — CONFIRMED
*cited as: Codex Sinaiticus*
- **Codex Sinaiticus:** “Και ηλθεν εις ναζαρα ου ην ανατεθραμμενος και εισηλθεν κατα το ειωθος αυτω εν τη ημερα των σαββατω εις την συναγωγην και ανεστη αναγνωναι”
- *Note:* Faithful rendering of aleph (q77f7vc4): 'kata to eiōthos autō' = according to his custom; 'tē hēmera tōn sabbatōn' rendered 'on the day of the Sabbaths' — plural kept. Quote [1] is a heading echo (commentary), not scripture.
- *Checked 2026-11-03 · editions fetched: sinaiticus*

## The editions used

- NETS (New English Translation of the Septuagint), Oxford master PDFs per book — https://ccat.sas.upenn.edu/nets/edition/ (the book's OT standard for THE BIBLICAL SABBATH)
- Codex Sinaiticus Greek readings + critical apparatus — https://greeknewtestament.net (per verse, aleph line + apparatus)
- Brenton Septuagint (1851 English), eBible.org — id eng-Brenton; like https://ebible.org/eng-Brenton/EXO20.htm
- Geneva Bible 1599, eBible.org — id enggnv; like https://ebible.org/enggnv/JHN03.htm
- King James Version (1769 standard text), eBible.org — id eng-kjv2006; like https://ebible.org/eng-kjv2006/PRO18.htm

Each verse stores its exact source page address in *verses.json*. See *README.md* for the standing rules and the one-line method for banking a new verse.
